# Shell v2 and the living component library - Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Rebuild the idlescape shell around the vendored shell v2 design - a persistent co-pilot bar, a 280px side panel with a pinnable XP tracker and a pop-out trace window, Automation with My tasks and Marketplace tabs, a cross-character Events panel, Characters merged into Account, a drawn SVG icon set - and land the component library that keeps it alive: token-driven class families, a styleguide section per family, screenshot baselines, and a test-time rule that forbids per-panel one-off styles.

**Architecture:** Three layers, bottom up. (1) *The token and class layer*: `web/src/styles/tokens.css` takes the bundle's values, `base.css` folds the bundle's base layer, `motion.css` holds the eight keyframes, and the stylesheet splits into one file per component family plus a `layout/` directory for per-surface geometry. A test-time rule (`web/src/styles/styles.test.ts`) pins each family file to its own class prefix, so there is nowhere to put a per-panel one-off style. (2) *The builder layer*: `web/src/ui/` gains `icon.ts` (the eleven glyphs), `parts.ts` (card, meter, segmented, sectionLabel, tag, dot, pill) beside the existing `el.ts`, and every panel composes those rather than emitting markup of its own. (3) *The surface layer*: three new frame-level singletons - a pairing store lifted out of the Claude panel, an append-only session-scoped event bus, and a trace window host beside `#bank-host` - feed the co-pilot bar, the Events panel and the canvas XP drop. The three structural migrations (Tasks to Automation, Characters into Account, Marketplace into a tab) land as one task because the eleven-glyph icon set only fits the strip after all three.

**Tech Stack:** Vite + vanilla TypeScript (`web/`), no framework and no React; panels built with `h()` from `web/src/ui/el.ts` or `innerHTML` templates; token-driven plain CSS in `web/src/styles/*.css`; Vitest + jsdom for unit tests; Playwright against the live stack for browser tests.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-06-idlescape-shell-v2-gaps-design.md`, the companion, which closes G1 to G9, fixes the order of work in its section 6 and records six rulings in its section 7. It defers to the design bundle on every literal. The design authority, in order:

1. `docs/design/idlescape-shell-v2/README.md` - what each surface is.
2. `docs/design/idlescape-shell-v2/Idlescape Shell v2.dc.html` - **every literal value**. All styles are inline. When a number is in question, it is the answer.
3. `docs/design/idlescape-shell-v2/tokens/*.css` and `components/**/*.{jsx,d.ts,prompt.md}` - the token layer and the per-component specs.
4. `docs/design/idlescape-shell-v2/design-system-readme.md` - the brand guide.
5. `.claude/skills/idlescape-design/SKILL.md` - the composition rules.

Session conventions: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-06-sprint-handoff.md` sections 1, 4 and 5. Sprint position: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-07-sprint-dragon-slayer.md` entry 2.

Four read-only maps were written for this plan and are cited throughout. They live at `docs/superpowers/maps/2026-09-07-shell-v2/`, promoted out of the plan's git-ignored SDD workspace on 2026-09-07 so a fresh clone has them (decision D65): `map-design.md` (the bundle, 1534 lines), `map-code.md` (the shell as built, 800 lines), `map-contracts.md` (G8.1 to G8.3 and the G1 to G3 data questions, 728 lines), `map-tests.md` (the test migration, 350 lines). **Read the map section a task cites before starting that task.** The maps quote literals so an implementer does not have to reopen the 80 KB mock for every number, but the mock still wins where they differ. They were written against `1bfdc7d`; where a map's line number and a grep at HEAD disagree, the grep is right, and the "Reconciled against HEAD on 2026-09-07" section at the end of this file lists every citation this plan has already corrected.

## Global Constraints

- **Vite plus vanilla TypeScript. No framework, no React.** The bundle's `components/*.jsx` are specifications to read, never code to import. Panels are built with `h()` from `web/src/ui/el.ts` or with `innerHTML` templates.
- Strict TypeScript, **no new `as any`**, **every file under 400 lines including test files and stylesheets** (split rather than trim comments), a `types.ts` per package, conventional commits.
- **One named exemption: `web/styleguide.html`.** It is 504 lines at HEAD and this plan roughly doubles it: eight new sections (`#badges`, `#alerts`, `#navigation`, `#icons`, `#copilot`, `#automation`, `#events`, plus `#hud` renamed to `#overlay`) and full-variant rewrites of `#buttons`, `#forms`, `#data`, `#frame` and `#panel`. The ceiling exists so a module stays readable and reviewable; the styleguide is a demo page with no logic, its value is that every family is on **one** scrollable page beside every other, and splitting it would put the demos further apart than the rule that binds them. It is exempt, with a cap of its own: `styleguide.families.test.ts` asserts it stays under **1,200** lines, and crossing that is the signal to split it into one partial per family under `web/styleguide/`, composed at build time, with `STYLEGUIDE_SECTIONS` pointing at the partial. No other file gets an exemption.
- **No em dashes in any new prose**: source comments, this plan, docs. Ruling R1 below carves out UI copy quoted from the mock, and confines it to named constants.
- **`localStorage` keys keep the `cs.` prefix and are never renamed.** Renaming one silently resets player state. This entry adds no new `cs.` key.
- **The strip stays generated from the plugin manifest**, `rebuildStrip()` contract unchanged. **Panels re-mount on every open**; `unmount()` releases every timer and subscription.
- **The Esc panic-key contract is unchanged**, including the iframe re-dispatch at `web/src/frame/stage.ts:195-206`.
- **ARIA**: the strip is a `tablist` with arrow keys; `#side-panel` stays `aria-live="polite"`; every icon-only button carries an `aria-label`; **one focus ring**, the orange `0 0 0 3px rgba(255,152,31,.18)`.
- Plugin **id `tasks` survives** the rename to Automation; only the display name changes. Panel id **`characters` is deleted**, with a permanent `characters -> account` fallback in the `cs.panel` resolver. **`marketplace` keeps its plugin and its view** and loses only its strip button. Events are **session-scoped and not persisted**.
- The product is **idlescape**, lowercase in UI strings, titles and wordmarks. Sentence case everywhere.
- **Motion is off under `prefers-reduced-motion`.**
- Branch: `sprint/dragon-slayer`. Every commit ends with:
  ```
  Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5.1 <noreply@anthropic.com>
  Claude-Session: 6cfc8eb7-fa0f-4398-8701-64d1005b1577
  ```
  Use `git -c core.safecrlf=false commit` and explicit `git add <paths>`, never `git add -A`.
- **`pwsh` is not installed.** Windows PowerShell 5.1 only: no `&&`, no ternary, no `?.`, no `??`.
- Per-command verification, from the repository root in Git Bash:
  - web: `cd web && npm run typecheck && npm run lint && npx vitest run`
  - e2e: stack up, then `cd web && npm run build:e2e && npx playwright test`. **Never a plain `npm run build`** for an e2e run, and never run Playwright from the repository root.
  - the whole gate: `scripts/verify.ps1`.
- **SP4b (sprint entry 1) landed into this same branch on 2026-09-07, and this plan has been reconciled against it.** The twelve `Reconcile against HEAD before dispatch` lines the plan carried (Tasks 1, 2, 3, 9, 10, 11, 12, 13, 14, 15, 16 and 18) are gone; what they asked for is done and recorded in "Reconciled against HEAD on 2026-09-07" at the end of this file. **Read that section before dispatching any task.** It covers seven more than the twelve, because the pass also gave the sprint board's open doors an owner: Tasks 7, 17 and 20 changed without ever having carried a line.

## What already shipped, verified in the code

Read this before Task 1. Line numbers were first anchored at `1bfdc7d` and re-anchored at `9608f2b` (HEAD, after SP4b) for every file the reconcile pass re-read. **Treat every line number in this plan as a hint and grep for the symbol beside it**; where a citation and a grep disagree, the grep is right. Source: `docs/superpowers/maps/2026-09-07-shell-v2/map-code.md`, plus the re-reads listed in "Reconciled against HEAD on 2026-09-07".

**The strip is derived, not declared.** `web/src/plugins/registry.ts:36-38` computes it as `order.filter(id => enabled.has(id) && plugins.get(id)!.panel).map(manifest)`. Registration order in `web/src/main.ts:196-240` is strip order. A plugin with no `panel()` never gets a button, which is why `status-hud` (`web/src/plugins/builtin/statusHud.ts:38-60`) has an `overlay()` and no strip presence. **Dropping `panel` from the marketplace plugin is therefore the whole of G4's strip half.**

**`rebuildStrip` destroys and rebuilds the whole strip.** `web/src/main.ts:243-259`: `strip.replaceChildren()`, then one raw `document.createElement('button')` per manifest with `btn.textContent = m.icon`, then `stripA11y.syncSelected()`, then `pluginOverlayHost.replaceChildren(...)`, then a re-open of whatever was open, then `runBanner.update(...)`. Anything painted onto a strip button (the run dot) has to be repainted after every registry change.

**`web/src/ui/strip.ts` (43 lines) is the whole strip a11y contract.** `applyTabListA11y(strip)` sets `role="tablist"` and `aria-orientation="vertical"`, and `syncSelected()` sets `role="tab"`, mirrors `.active` into `aria-selected`, applies a roving `tabIndex`, and **moves `title` into `data-tip` plus `aria-label`** then removes `title`. Arrow, Home and End move focus only; activation is the click delegation in `frame/panels.ts`.

**`web/src/frame/panels.ts` (64 lines) is the whole panel host and the `cs.panel` resolver.** `open(id)` returns silently when `views.get(id)` is missing (line 29). `restore()` reads `cs.panel` and **only opens when `views.has(saved)`** (line 60), so a stored `characters` already opens nothing today. `open()` writes `cs.panel` and `close()` removes it. That one function is where the permanent fallback goes, and nowhere else.

**`web/src/ui/el.ts` (51 lines) is the whole builder set**: `h`, `kv`, `badge`, `alert`, `empty`. There is no `card`, `section`, `meter`, `segmented` or `dot` builder; `section()` is a private helper inside `web/src/plugins/builtin/tasks.ts:57-59`.

**The `.p-*` dialect is concentrated in eleven files.** `data.css` and `button.css` define most of the thirty `.p-*` classes as **aliases beside a newer unprefixed class** (`.kv, .p-row`, `.table, .p-table`, `.empty, .p-empty`, `.alert, .p-msg`, `.btn, .p-btn`, `.muted, .p-muted`). Every file that uses `h()` already emits the unprefixed names; every file that builds DOM by `innerHTML` emits the prefixed ones. The prefixed emitters are `panels/account.ts`, `panels/config.ts`, `panels/connect.ts`, `panels/connectCard.ts`, `builtin/characters.ts`, `builtin/lootTracker.ts`, `builtin/xpTracker.ts`, `builtin/notes.ts`, `builtin/screenshot.ts`, `plugins/pluginsPanel.ts`, `plugins/settingsForm.ts` **plus four the "eleven" count misses**: `builtin/bank.ts:40`, `builtin/tasks.ts:90,168`, `builtin/tasksViews.ts:225,227,228` and `builtin/traceView.ts:135`, all `p-btn` or `p-input`. Fifteen files; `grep -rn "\bp-[a-z]" web/src` is the list, and Task 3's own offenders test walks the `plugins` root, so it reports the last four whether or not the Files list names them. That split is the conversion seam.

**One `.p-` selector also survives in the stylesheet layer outside the alias files**: `tasks.css:68`, `.trace-head .p-input`, added by SP4b Task 11's trace head. It follows `traceView.ts`'s own `p-input` through Task 3 and is the one `.p-` rule the Task 2 split moves into `layout/automation.css` rather than into `legacy.css`.

**The stylesheet layer is 1,004 lines across 13 files**, largest `bank.css` at 268, `tasks.css` 174, `data.css` 126, `tokens.css` 85. **`index.css` imports `tasks.css` twice** (lines 8 and 14). `tasks.css` grew from 108 to 174 under SP4b: the run card's detail lines and health list (`.run-detail-line`, `.run-health`, `.run-health-item`), the per-script toggle (`.task-row-toggle`), the whole `.report*` block (lines 42 to 66, 24 rules), and the banner's second row (`.run-banner-detail`, `.run-banner-detail-toggle`, `.run-banner-health`, the `flex-wrap: wrap` on `.run-banner` and the four `.has-detail` pill offsets at lines 120 to 128).

**`frame/stage.ts:241-247` hardcodes the strip and panel widths as numbers** (`:243`, `reserved = 32 + (panelOpen && stage.clientWidth > 1100 ? 210 : 0) + 16`), duplicating `--strip-w` and `--panel-w`. Moving the panel to 280 and the strip to 40 in CSS alone silently desyncs canvas sizing.

**`main.ts:50-51` reads `cs.size` and `cs.filter` unguarded at module scope**, which throws outright in a private window with site data blocked. Every other `cs.` access in the codebase is wrapped in try/catch.

**XP and loot are edge-driven, not sampled.** `web/src/sessions/wire.ts:31-36` subscribes `hooks.on('xp', ev => tracker.onXp(ev, now()))` and `:37` `hooks.on('inventory', ...)`. `XpEvent` is `{ skill: number; xp: number; level: number; delta: number }` (`web/src/clientTypes.ts:55`). The 5 s and 2 s timers in `builtin/xpTracker.ts:7,35` and `builtin/lootTracker.ts:7,82` are **render** timers, not samplers, and both panels rebuild `body.innerHTML` wholesale on every tick.

**`CharacterSession` already carries `startedAt` and `lastStateAt`** (`web/src/sessions/types.ts:20-21`), stamped at `manager.ts:170` and refreshed at `:106-107`. Neither has a consumer. `startedAt` means "the iframe object was created", in state `booting`, which is **not** the online clock the design asks for.

**The pairing truth lives inside one panel's mount.** The only `agentTokens` subscription in the codebase is created at `web/src/panels/connect.ts:212-217` and torn down at `:244-245`. With the Claude panel closed, nothing in the frame knows whether Claude is paired. The status line the shell paints today is a hard-coded literal, written at `web/src/sessions/wire.ts:40` and `web/src/frame/stage.ts:230`.

**Bank icons already reach the frame with no new client patch.** `web/src/main.ts:209` builds `createIconCache({ client: () => stage.sessions.active()?.hooks ?? null })` in the parent document. The iframe is same-origin (`/play.html`), so `getObjIcon(id, 1)` is an ordinary synchronous method call, and IndexedDB (`idlescape-icons`) carries the cache across reloads when no client is open. Client patch numbering stays at **28**.

**`RunStatus.attached` is hardcoded `false`** in four places (`tasks/api.ts:120` and `:139`, `agent/workerHost.ts:78`, `tasks/api.harness.ts:24`), so the banner's "Claude is attached" mark at `runBanner.ts:169` can never render.

**Three divergent copies of the run-state vocabulary now exist**, which is one more than the plan was written against: `frame/runBanner.ts:49-62` (`message`) plus `:78-83` (`tone`), `builtin/tasksViews.ts:31-48` (`statusLabel`), and SP4b Task 11's `builtin/runReportView.ts:22` (`STATUS_TONE`, over `RunSummary.status`, which is a `RunState`). They disagree in wording and in tone for the same state: `stopped` is `neutral` in all three but `failed` is `error` in `statusLabel` and `error` in `STATUS_TONE` while the banner reaches `error` only through `state === 'failed' || reason === 'hard-stop'`. Ruling R22 unifies the first two; `STATUS_TONE` reads a finished run's outcome and is deliberately left alone (see Task 14 Step 7).

**Every trace event already carries a `kind`**, stamped as `data-kind` at `builtin/traceView.ts:63` and filtered on at `:82-86`. The design's 2px kind rails are therefore pure CSS, no data change. **The same line also stamps `data-line`**, added by SP4b Task 11 for the copy button's visible-lines filter, so Task 16's substring filter has the attribute it needs already.

**The suite, at `9608f2b`:** 107 unit test files, 6 harnesses, 10 Playwright spec files. Six files are at or over 350 lines and four of them are in this redesign's path. At HEAD: `web/src/plugins/builtin/tasks.ts` **399**, one line under the ceiling and the single tightest file in this plan's path; `sessions/manager.test.ts` 396, `plugins/builtin/tasks.test.ts` **394** (SP4b's Tasks 9 to 11 grew it back past the 356 it was cut to), `e2e/bank-ui.pw.test.ts` 380, `bank/view.test.ts` 357, `tasks/api.ts` 393. Add to those two files this plan grows without a ceiling of their own: `web/src/main.ts` at 358, which ten tasks here add to, and `web/styleguide.html` at 504, which is already over. All are handled: `main.ts` gives up its register block to `frame/registerPanels.ts` in Task 10 and its singletons to `frame/singletons.ts` in Task 11, `tasks.ts` gives up its history-and-report block in Task 10 Step 0b, `tasks.test.ts` splits in Task 10 Step 0, and `styleguide.html` takes the one named exemption in the Global Constraints.

## Plan rulings

Thirty-eight decisions: the companion's six restated as C1 to C6, plus thirty-two new ones, where the design bundle, the companion and the code do not line up, or where the plan had to choose. Each says what it costs if it is wrong. The companion's own six rulings are restated first, as C1 to C6, because an implementer reads this file and not that one.

### The companion's six, restated

**C1. The product is idlescape**, lowercase in UI strings, titles and wordmarks. The bundle arrived calling it "fable"; every occurrence was renamed on vendoring and the placeholder host `fable.scotho.com` became `osrs.scotho.com`. See `docs/design/idlescape-shell-v2/PROVENANCE.md`. *Cost if wrong:* a wordmark that says the wrong product name.

**C2. The plugin id `tasks` survives the rename to Automation.** Only the display name changes. The id appears in `cs.panel` values, `[data-panel="tasks"]` selectors in both suites, and `cs.plugin.tasks` settings docs. *Cost if wrong:* a silent settings reset for every existing player, plus a broken strip dot selector.

**C3. `characters` as a panel id is deleted**, with a **permanent** `characters -> account` fallback in the `cs.panel` resolver. Permanent, not a migration step: the key is never rewritten, so an old value can arrive at any time. *Cost if wrong:* a returning player's shell opens no panel at all.

**C4. `marketplace` survives as a plugin and a view** and loses only its strip button. *Cost if wrong:* a deleted plugin id that `cs.plugin.marketplace` documents still name.

**C5. Events are session-scoped and not persisted**, per the panel's own footer copy ("this session"). *Cost if wrong:* a migration and a quota question this entry does not want.

**C6. The mock is the source of truth for every literal.** Where the companion and the mock disagree about a number, the mock wins and the companion is wrong. *Cost if wrong:* a shell that is close to the design instead of being the design.

### New rulings

**R1. The mock's UI copy is a design literal, not new prose, and ships verbatim including its em dashes.** The sprint constraint forbids em dashes in new prose; the mock's final copy contains **twelve** strings that carry one: `grep -c '—'` over `Idlescape Shell v2.dc.html` returns 12 at `1bfdc7d`, and `map-design.md` note S1's "at least 13" is one out. The authority order puts the mock above every other document for literals, and copy is explicitly called final by `README.md` under "Fidelity". The no-em-dash rule therefore binds source comments, plan and spec prose, and any copy this plan invents, and does not bind copy quoted from the mock.
**The hedge, which is mandatory:** every mock string containing an em dash is declared as an exported `const` in `web/src/ui/copy.ts`, and `web/src/ui/copy.test.ts` enumerates all of them. `copy.ts` ships **eight plain constants plus four templates**, which is the twelve. If the owner reverses this ruling, it is one file and one commit.
*Cost if wrong:* twelve string edits in one module, and a screenshot baseline refresh.

**R2. The v2 tokens take the bundle's names, and every legacy call site is frozen to its literal in the same commit.** Nine tokens keep an existing name in `web/src/styles/tokens.css` while changing value: `--sp-1` 4 to 2, `--sp-2` 8 to 3, `--sp-3` 12 to 4, `--sp-4` 16 to 6, `--sp-5` 24 to 8, `--sp-6` 32 to 10, `--fs-base` 13 to 12, `--fs-md` 12 to 12.5, `--lh` 1.4 to 1.45, plus `--ctl-h` 28 to 27, `--ctl-h-sm` 24 to 25, `--ctl-h-lg` 36 to 29, `--panel-w` 210 to 280, `--strip-w` 32 to 40, and `--focus-ring` and `--ease` change shape entirely. There are 145 `var(--sp-N)` call sites and 28 `var(--radius)` call sites in the shipped stylesheets at HEAD (136 and 26 before SP4b grew `tasks.css`). A naive port shifts every one of them silently. **Ruling:** Task 1 writes the v2 values under the bundle's names **and** rewrites every existing call site in the not-yet-converted stylesheets to the literal px value it renders today, so the legacy CSS is frozen at its current appearance while the token names carry v2 values. Each surface re-adopts the tokens when its own task converts it. A test asserts that no unconverted stylesheet contains `var(--sp-`, `var(--fs-`, `var(--ctl-h`, `var(--lh)` or `var(--ease)`.

**Three names the naive reading of this ruling misses, and they are the ones that bite:**
- `--title-h` and `--foot-h` already exist in `tokens.css:65-66` at `22px` and `18px`, and `frame.css:5` and `:50` already read them. Step 3 gives them the mock's `28px` and `21px`, so they are frozen in Step 6's table like every other changed name, and the `CHANGED` regex names them.
- `var(--focus-ring)` has **six** call sites at HEAD, not one and not the five this ruling first counted: `base.css:26`, `bank.css:147`, `frame.css:44` and `tabs.css:7` (both `inset var(--focus-ring)`), and **two** in `tasks.css`, `:143` (`.run-banner-line:focus-visible`) and `:161` (`.run-banner-detail-toggle:focus-visible`, added by SP4b Task 10). The single-shadow v2 value behaves differently inset from the current two-ring value, so the five legacy sites are frozen to today's literal in Step 6 and re-adopt the token when their own task converts them. `base.css` is the one site that takes the new ring at Task 1, deliberately: it is the one focus ring the design brief specifies.

*Cost if wrong:* the alternative is a single 3,000-line commit that swaps tokens and rewrites every stylesheet at once, which no reviewer can gate.

**R3. `--panel-w` and `--strip-w` stay the single source of truth, and `frame/stage.ts` reads them once with a checked fallback.** `stage.ts:242` hardcodes 32 and 210. The two width declarations in `frame.css` (`.side-panel { width }` and `.icon-strip { width }`) are therefore the **named exceptions** to R2's freeze: they take `var(--panel-w)` and `var(--strip-w)` at Task 1, in the same commit that teaches the canvas to reserve them, so the chrome and the reserved width never disagree. Reading `getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--panel-w')` is correct in a browser and returns the empty string in jsdom, so the module keeps numeric fallbacks `PANEL_W = 280` and `STRIP_W = 40` and a unit test reads `tokens.css` and asserts the fallbacks equal the token literals. *Cost if wrong:* canvas sizing desyncs from the panel by 70px, which the `bank-ui.pw` clamp test would eventually catch and blame on the bank.

**R4. `failed` borrows the stuck chrome for `LINGER_MS` after it settled, then falls to standby or unpaired.** The mock has five presented states and none is "failed". The banner already lingers a settled run for `LINGER_MS = 4000` (`runBanner.ts:34,37`), measured from when it **settled**, which is a stamp the bar keeps in its own closure. `RunStatus` carries no such field and `startedAt` is not one, so `RunPresentationInput` takes `settledAt: number | null` and the bar fills it; keying the window off `startedAt` would give a run that lasted longer than the window no stuck chrome at all. `done` and `stopped` do not linger on the bar at all; their news belongs in the toast the banner already fires. *Cost if wrong:* a failure scrolls past before the player reads it, or a finished run keeps the bar hostage.

**R5. A character that is not logged in shows the unpaired or standby chrome with the primary button disabled and the message replaced by the existing session wording.** `RunStatus` is `idle` for the whole of a session's title-screen life, so rows 9 and 10 of the state table would otherwise offer "Run a script" to a character sitting on the login screen. The replacement copy is **moved, not invented**: the four strings already at `frame/stage.ts:231-235`. *Cost if wrong:* a button that produces `not_signed_in` when pressed.

**R6. Pairing never overrides a live run, and the canvas pairing pill may legitimately disagree with the bar.** A player-started run on an unpaired account is a running run; the mock's own `runExists` is derived from run state alone. The pill keeps the pairing truth, so `● claude driving` appears only when the account is paired. *Cost if wrong:* the shell claims Claude is driving a character Claude cannot see.

**R7. G2's premise is false and the canvas XP drop feeds off `hooks.on('xp')` directly.** The companion says the XP tracker "samples state on a timer" and proposes a client state diff. There is no sampling window: `HookEvents.xp` is one discrete `XpEvent { skill, xp, level, delta }` per xp change (`clientTypes.ts:55`), and `createXpTracker.onXp` is edge-driven off it at `sessions/wire.ts:31-36`. No state diff is built, and the level-up producer comes free from the same event's `level` field. *Cost if wrong:* none known; this makes the work smaller than the companion budgets for.

**R8. G3 gets a new `onlineSince: number | null` on `CharacterSession`, stamped inside `refresh()`.** The companion says no per-character session-start timestamp exists; `startedAt` has existed since SP7 but means "iframe created", in state `booting`, so a frame parked on the title screen for two hours would read `2:00:00`. The stamp goes at `sessions/manager.ts:106-107`, beside `lastStateAt`, on the transition into `online`, and is cleared on every transition out. That single site covers the `login`, `logout` and `disconnect` hooks and the 1 s poll, because all four call `refresh`. Frame-local, never persisted. *Cost if wrong:* the tab clock counts frame age instead of play time.

**R9. The Events feed attaches per session at `frame/stage.ts`, not to the tasks router.** `tasks/router.ts:174-175` (the two `if (deps.activeId() === characterId)` guards; grep for that string, not the line) drops both status and trace for background sessions by design, so a cross-character feed cannot subscribe to `deps.tasks`. The run producer subscribes to the raw per-session `TasksApi` at the point `tasksBySession.set(session.id, api)` already happens. *Cost if wrong:* the Events panel shows nothing for any character that is not in front, which is exactly the thing it exists to do.

**R10. The pairing subscription and the bank store are lifted to `main.ts` frame-lifetime singletons.** `agentTokens` is subscribed inside the Claude panel's `mount()` and destroyed in `unmount()`; `bankStore.start()/.stop()` are called by the bank **window**. An append-only feed fed by producers that only run while the player is looking at them shows a hole in the middle of every session, and the co-pilot bar needs the pairing truth with the panel closed. Both become singletons; the panel and the window become consumers. *Cost if wrong:* the co-pilot bar cannot render `unpaired` versus `standby` at all, and two of six event types go quiet.

**R11. The `claude` event chip is scoped to three producers, and this entry builds no message channel.** The mock's three `claude` rows are "MacBook session paired" (from `agentTokens`), "Gateway connected - ws ok" (from the `/health` poll) and "Claude started Chop and drop" (from `run_started.startedBy === 'claude'`). There is no agent transport into the web app; the SP4c tab socket is future work. *Cost if wrong:* a chip that filters an empty set, which is a visible defect the empty state already handles.

**R12. The trace window follows the active character and closes on a tab switch.** It is a stage overlay, and the router drops background events, so anything else needs a per-session event subscription the router does not expose. This matches what `tasks.ts` does today on `onActiveChanged`. *Cost if wrong:* a window showing a frozen trace for a character the player left.

**R13. The trace window owns its own element reference and is planned against `openReportFor`, not `openTraceFor`.** Confirmed at HEAD: SP4b Task 11 has landed, `openTraceFor` is gone, `openReportFor` is `builtin/tasks.ts:257`, and the panel's live-append lookup is now `reportHost.firstElementChild` at `tasks.ts:287`, guarded by `openReport === api.status().runId`. `renderTraceView` therefore has two mounts already: the report's, behind `runReportView.ts:135-145`'s `traceToggle`, and this window's. *Cost if wrong:* live trace rows append into the wrong window, or into none.

**R14. No new client patch. Numbering stays at 28.** G8.3's "if it is not reachable, that is a client patch on the critical path" does not fire: `bank/icons.ts` is already a frame-side module reading `stage.sessions.active()?.hooks` across a same-origin iframe, with IndexedDB behind it. The one caveat the bank window must carry is that with no character open at all, `deps.client()` is `null` and uncached objs render with no icon, so the empty slot must be legible without one. *Cost if wrong:* nothing; this ruling removes scope rather than adding it.

**R15. There are two empty-state patterns, not one.** README calls the loot empty state "the pattern for all empty states"; the Events empty state differs on all eight of its metrics (34px versus 40px circle, `⌕` versus `◌`, no `bob`, 12.5 versus 13.5px title, 180 versus 190px copy, `gap:7px` versus `9px`, `padding:28px 12px` versus `36px 12px`, a grey 24px button versus a primary 27px one). The mock wins. Both ship, as `.empty-state` and `.empty-state.compact`. *Cost if wrong:* one of the two empty states is visibly off-design.

**R16. Where README and the mock disagree on a number, the mock's number ships.** The three that matter: the run card's elapsed is **16px**, not README's 14px (14 is the co-pilot bar's); history row meta is **not** mono and carries no `font-variant-numeric`, despite README's "mono meta"; the Automation card buttons are `height:25px` (`sm`), not `xs`. `map-design.md` section 5.2 lists all fourteen. *Cost if wrong:* three wrong numbers on the most-looked-at surface in the shell.

**R17. Four badge geometries ship as three classes, not four families.** The Badge component is `height:17; radius:9; font-weight:650; padding:0 7px`. The run card's badge is `height:19; radius:10; font-weight:700; padding:0 8px; letter-spacing:.03em`; the script card badge is `height:16; radius:8; font-weight:650` (Tag geometry with Badge colours); the event character badge is `height:15; radius:8; font-size:9px`. Ruling: `.badge` is the component, `.badge-lg` is the run card's, `.badge-sm` covers both the script card and the event character badge at `height:16`/`radius:8` with the event one taking `.badge-sm.badge-quiet` for its 9px type and `#2c2c2e` ground. Four geometries, one family, three modifiers. *Cost if wrong:* one badge is a pixel out.

**R18. The roughly twenty untokenised hex values in the mock become tokens, added to `tokens.css` under new names.** `map-design.md` note S6 lists them: `#1b1b1d`, `#28282a`, `#2a2a2c`, `#2c2c2e`, `#3e3e3c`, `#666`, `#9a9a98`, `#a0a09e`, `#f07370`, `#2e7d32`, `#ffff00`, `#00ff80`, `#252220`, `#2c2925`, `#33302c`, `#171513`, `#4e4a44`, `#141414`, `#191919`, `#0a0a0a`, `#0d0d0d`. A token port that copies only `tokens/colors.css` leaves all of them hard-coded in the CSS, which is exactly the drift the design system exists to prevent. *Cost if wrong:* a future palette change misses twenty values.

**R19. The decorative `⚠` is dropped; the `✓`, `∞`, `◌`, `⌕`, `●`, `×` and `+` glyphs stay.** The brand guide is unambiguous ("No emoji. Ever. Unicode glyphs only as data"), and the mock's `⚠` in front of the My tasks requirement line is decorative, which `map-design.md` note S23 flags as directly contradicting it. The requirement line keeps its exact geometry, colour and copy and loses only the glyph, which also matches README's own description ("amber requirement line"). The `✓` in the done quest-step marker and in `Added ✓` are state marks inside drawn controls, in the same class as the `∞` bank tab; they stay. *Cost if wrong:* one glyph, either direction.

**R20. The `characters -> account` fallback is a raw-string map in `restore()`, because `PanelId` no longer contains `characters`.** `PanelId` (`web/src/types.ts:5`) is a closed union; deleting `characters` from it is the compile error that finds the three literal use sites (`stage.ts:140`, `main.ts:286`, `main.ts:298`). The stored value, though, is an arbitrary string from an old browser, so the fallback maps `string -> PanelId | null` before the `views.has` check. *Cost if wrong:* a type assertion hides the one case the fallback exists for.

**R21. The co-pilot bar renders no "Claude is attached" mark, and this entry does not fix `RunStatus.attached`.** It is hardcoded `false` in four places; the banner's `✦` at `runBanner.ts:169` has never rendered. Pairing comes from the pairing store (R10) instead, which is the correct source anyway. *Cost if wrong:* a mark nobody has seen stays unseen.

**R22. One run-state vocabulary, in one module.** `web/src/frame/runPresentation.ts` exports `presentRun(input): RunPresentation`, and both the co-pilot bar and the run card's `statusLabel` consume it. Today there are two divergent copies of the same strings and SP4b adds a third. *Cost if wrong:* the bar says "You took over" while the card says "Paused: you took control, resumes in Ns", which is the current behaviour and is a bug.

**R23. The stuck reason line reads a condition-to-copy table, and this entry does not widen SP4b's `RunStatus.health`.** Confirmed at HEAD: `health: { condition: HealthCondition; since: number } | null` is `tasks/types.ts:311`, which gives `no-progress`, not the mock's `no Tree within 12 tiles`. `HealthEvent.detail?: string` exists (`types.ts:108`) and reaches the trace and the run card's health notes (`tasksViews.ts:70-73` prints it) but never reaches `RunStatus`. Ruling: `presentRun` picks the reason line in priority order `health.condition` mapped through a nine-entry copy table, then `status.statusLine.trim() || null`, then `'no reason reported'`. The `trim() ||` matters: `statusLine` is `string` and not `string | null` (`tasks/types.ts:302`), and its "nothing to say" value is the empty string, which would otherwise render as a blank second line. *Cost if wrong:* the reason line is a category rather than a sentence. Approved as decision D13; carrying the detail sentence is on SP4c's list.

**R24. The bar replaces `#run-banner` with `#copilot-bar`.** The element is 42px and persistent, not a hidden banner, so the id changes with the surface. `web/e2e/tasks.pw.test.ts`'s three banner steps and the whole of `web/src/frame/runBanner.test.ts` are rewritten in the same task. The `[data-panel="tasks"] .strip-dot` selector survives untouched. *Cost if wrong:* one selector rename across two files.

**R25. The co-pilot bar re-earns every live-region property the run banner had.** `#run-banner` is `role="status"` and `runBanner.test.ts:108-157` is built entirely around that region being atomic: `aria-hidden="true"` on the elapsed and countdown nodes, and same-node identity checks so a per-second tick does not re-announce the whole bar. Nothing in the design brief or the companion mentions this, so deleting `runBanner.test.ts` deletes the only record that the contract exists. The bar keeps `role="status"`, keeps both `aria-hidden` nodes, and keeps the `signature()` diff. *Cost if wrong:* a screen reader reads the entire bar once a second, forever.

**R26. The `.card.html` files become the variant checklist; the screenshot baselines are taken of the styleguide.** The sprint asks for the five `components/**/*.card.html` files as Playwright screenshot baselines. They cannot render: each references `../../_ds_bundle.js`, which is not in the vendored bundle, plus React and Babel from unpkg, which the project does not load (`map-design.md` note S26). Ruling: a unit test parses each `.card.html` for the component names and variants it exercises and asserts the corresponding styleguide section contains one demo per variant; a Playwright spec screenshots the five styleguide sections against committed baselines. The Navigation card's nine strip entries versus the mock's eleven (note S9) is resolved in favour of the mock; the checklist test reads the card for *component* names, not strip order. *Cost if wrong:* the baselines pin our rendering of the system rather than the bundle's, which is the only rendering that exists.

**R27. UI state that the mock keeps in its own state object lives in the panel's `panel()` closure, not in `localStorage`.** The Automation tab (`my` / `market`), the Events panel's three filters and the pinned-tracker flag are all UI state the mock holds in memory. The constraint list sanctions no new `cs.` key and every existing one is player state, so none of these gets one. The lifecycle makes it work: the `PanelView` object is built **once** per plugin enable (`registry.ts:47`) and only its `mount`/`unmount` pair runs per open (`frame/panels.ts:27-40`), so a closure variable survives a re-open and dies with a sign-out. *Cost if wrong:* the Automation tab and the event filters reset on a page reload, which is the same behaviour as the Events feed itself, and which ruling C5 already accepts.

**R28. The gate screen's `.card` is renamed before `card.css` takes the name.** `layout.css:15` already defines `.card` as the full-screen centred card of the gate, home and character-creation screens, and Task 6's panel card would silently win on padding, background, border and radius. The whole screen family moves off the `card` prefix to `.screen*` (Task 2 Step 0) rather than being renamed within it, because `card.css` owns every `card-*` class under the styles rule and the screen chrome is per-surface geometry, not a library component. *Cost if wrong:* three player-facing screens no test covers quietly take panel chrome.

**R29. The run card's quest-step row renders `done` and `current` only, because `upcoming` is not derivable in the frame.** `Script.tasks` is the ordered list, but `TasksApiDeps` narrows the library to manifests on purpose (`tasks/api.ts:87-91`: "the api never needs a script's `tasks`") and the executable `Script` lives in the Worker. The reachable source is the `task_enter` trace the recorder already keeps, which knows what has happened and not what is next. The `upcoming` marker still ships in the CSS and on the styleguide, because the mock draws it and a replayed finished run has one. *Cost if wrong:* the row is shorter than the mock's; the alternative is a new frame-to-Worker contract this entry does not want.

**R30. The Esc panic key always wins, and no surface added by this plan calls `preventDefault` on it.** The trace window gains an Escape close (Task 16) while the co-pilot bar keeps the run banner's document keydown (Task 14) and `frame/stage.ts:195-206` keeps the iframe re-dispatch. Every one of those handlers follows the convention `bank/view.ts:205` already sets, `if (event.key !== 'Escape' || event.defaultPrevented) return;`, and none of them preventDefaults. One Escape over a live run with the trace open therefore closes the window **and** pauses the run. *Cost if wrong:* a window that swallowed Escape would silently disable the one interaction the sprint says must not move, and nothing would fail.

**R31. `web/styleguide.html` is the single named exemption from the 400-line ceiling, capped at 1,200 instead.** It is 504 lines today and this plan roughly doubles it. The ceiling exists so a module stays reviewable; the styleguide is a demo page with no logic whose whole value is that every family sits on one scrollable page beside every other. `styleguide.families.test.ts` enforces the 1,200 cap, and crossing it is the signal to split into one partial per family. *Cost if wrong:* one long HTML file, or a build-time composition step this entry has not budgeted.

**R32. `main.ts` gives up its register block and its singletons rather than growing past the ceiling.** It is 358 lines and ten tasks here add to it. Task 10 moves the `shell.register` block to `web/src/frame/registerPanels.ts` (which is also what makes the eleven-button strip order testable, since `main.ts`'s module body is not executable from a unit test), and Task 11 moves the frame-lifetime singletons to `web/src/frame/singletons.ts`. *Cost if wrong:* some task around 14 stops to do a file split it has no reason to be doing, in a commit about something else.


---

## File structure

Paths are repository-relative. Line counts are targets that keep every file under the 400-line ceiling.

### Create - the stylesheet layer (Tasks 1 to 3)

- `web/src/styles/motion.css` - the eight keyframes and the reduced-motion block. ~30 lines.
- `web/src/styles/badge.css` - `.badge`, `.badge-*` tones and sizes, `.tag`, `.dot` family. ~70 lines.
- `web/src/styles/card.css` - `.card`, `.card-hero`, `.card-rail-*`, `.kv`, `.section-label`. ~70 lines.
- `web/src/styles/meter.css` - `.meter`, `.meter-fill`, tones, `.shimmer`. ~40 lines.
- `web/src/styles/alert.css` - `.alert`, `.empty`, `.empty-state`, `.toast`. ~90 lines.
- `web/src/styles/overlay.css` - `.ov-pill`, `.hud-pill`, `.xp-drop`, the `.window` chrome with its warm and placement modifiers, and the `.menu` classes (D42). ~140 lines.
- `web/src/styles/strip.css` - `.icon-strip`, `.strip-btn`, `.strip-sep`, `.strip-dot`. ~50 lines.
- `web/src/styles/tab.css` - `.char-tabs`, `.char-tab`, `.seg`, `.seg-btn`, `.chip`. ~90 lines.
- `web/src/styles/icon.css` - `.icon` sizing and colour inheritance. ~15 lines.
- `web/src/styles/layout/frame.css` - the frame regions only. ~120 lines.
- `web/src/styles/layout/copilot.css`, `layout/automation.css`, `layout/events.css`, `layout/trace.css`, `layout/bank.css`, `layout/panels.css`, `layout/dialog.css`.
- `web/src/styles/families.ts` - the family-to-prefix table the styles rule reads.
- `web/src/styles/styles.test.ts` - the rule with teeth (line ceiling, prefix discipline, single import, no `.p-`).

### Modify - the stylesheet layer

- `web/src/styles/tokens.css` - the v2 token layer. Grows from 85 to ~150 lines.
- `web/src/styles/base.css` - the bundle's base layer folded in, keeping the existing reset, `.hidden`, `.sr-only`, `.num`, `.pixel` and `kbd`.
- `web/src/styles/index.css` - the import manifest, each file once.
- `web/src/styles/button.css`, `forms.css` - reshaped to the v2 families; `data.css`, `tasks.css`, `frame.css`, `tabs.css`, `overlays.css`, `dialog.css`, `bank.css` are split into the files above and deleted.
- `web/src/styles/bank.test.ts` - repointed at `layout/bank.css`; split into `bank.test.ts` and `bank.selectors.test.ts` if it passes 350 lines.

### Create - builders and frame modules

- `web/src/ui/icon.ts` - `icon(name, size?)`, `ICON_NAMES`, the `IconName` union, the eleven paths.
- `web/src/ui/parts.ts` - `card`, `sectionLabel`, `tag`, `dot`, `meter`, `segmented`, `chip`, `pill`, `hudPill`, `emptyState`, `menu`.
- `web/src/ui/copy.ts` - every mock string containing an em dash (ruling R1).
- `web/src/plugins/builtin/tasksHistory.ts` - the history, report and trace block lifted out of `builtin/tasks.ts` before Task 10 adds to it, exporting `createHistorySection(deps)`. `tasks.ts` is 399 lines at HEAD, one under the ceiling, and this plan adds to it in Tasks 10, 15 and 16. Created by Task 10 Step 0b. ~150 lines.
- `web/src/panels/config.test.ts` - the Configuration panel has no test file at all today; Task 20 creates one for its two reserved sections and its four controls. ~60 lines.
- `web/src/frame/registerPanels.ts` - the `shell.register` block lifted out of `main.ts`, exporting `PANEL_ORDER` and `registerShellPanels(shell, deps)`. Created by Task 10. ~90 lines.
- `web/src/frame/registerPanels.test.ts` - feeds a recording fake shell and asserts the eleven ids, in order.
- `web/src/frame/singletons.ts` - the frame-lifetime singletons (`createFrameSingletons({ uid })`: the pairing store, the bank store, the event bus, the `/health` poll and the producer wiring), lifted out of `main.ts`. Created by Task 11. ~120 lines.
- `web/src/frame/pairing.ts` - the frame-level pairing store lifted out of `panels/connect.ts`.
- `web/src/frame/events.ts` - the session-scoped append-only event ring buffer.

- `web/src/frame/runPresentation.ts` - `presentRun`, the one run-state vocabulary (R22).

- `web/src/plugins/builtin/eventsViews.ts` - its row, chip and empty-state rendering.

- Tests beside each, plus `web/src/panels/account.characters.test.ts` (the 15 tests moved out of `builtin/characters.test.ts`).

### Create - regression fixtures

- `web/e2e/styleguide.pw.test.ts` and `web/e2e/styleguide.pw.test.ts-snapshots/` - eight baselines, one per styleguide section the five demo cards cover.
- `web/src/styleguide.families.test.ts` - the `.card.html` variant checklist and the styleguide completeness rule.

### Modify - the shell

- `web/src/types.ts` - `PanelId` loses `characters`, gains `events`.
- `web/src/plugins/types.ts` - `PluginManifest['icon']` narrows to `IconName`.
- `web/src/frame/panels.ts` - the permanent fallback in `restore()`.
- `web/src/main.ts` - the guarded `cs.` reads, the trace host, the bar. **It is 358 lines at `1bfdc7d` and ten tasks in this plan add to it**, so it does not keep the register list or the singletons: Task 10 moves the register block to `frame/registerPanels.ts` and Task 11 moves the frame-lifetime singletons to `frame/singletons.ts`, and `main.ts` calls each once. Without those two extractions it crosses the 400-line ceiling somewhere around Task 14, in a task that has no reason to be doing a file split.
- `web/src/partials/frame.html` - `#copilot-bar`, `#trace-host`, the panel header's Pin button, the pinned tracker host.
- `web/src/frame/stage.ts` - geometry from tokens, the per-session event producers, the pairing pill source.
- `web/src/frame/characterTabs.ts` - the online clock and its in-place tick.

- `web/src/sessions/types.ts`, `sessions/manager.ts` - `onlineSince`.
- `web/src/sessions/wire.ts` - the xp and inventory producers, the pairing pill.
- `web/src/plugins/builtin/tasks.ts`, `tasksViews.ts`, `marketplace.ts` - Automation, its tabs and the run card patch. **Moving under SP4b.**
- `web/src/plugins/builtin/traceView.ts` - `Copy for Claude`, the `mm:ss.d` offset, the raw kind token. **Moving under SP4b.**
- `web/src/plugins/builtin/xpTracker.ts`, `lootTracker.ts`, `notes.ts`, `screenshot.ts`, `bank.ts`, `plugins/pluginsPanel.ts`, `plugins/settingsForm.ts`, `panels/account.ts`, `panels/config.ts`, `panels/connect.ts`, `panels/connectCard.ts` - `.p-*` retirement and the v2 surfaces.

- `web/styleguide.html`, `web/src/styleguide.ts` - a section per family. **`styleguide.ts:3,82-88` imports `createRunBanner` and drives the four `[data-sg-banner]` demos with the real renderer**, so Task 14's deletion of `runBanner.ts` breaks the build here unless that task edits both files in the same commit; it does.

### Task 1: The v2 token layer, the base fold, and the frame geometry

The bundle's four token files become one `tokens.css`; `tokens/base.css` folds into `base.css`; the eight keyframes get their own file. Nine tokens change value under a name the shipped stylesheets already use, so this task also freezes every legacy call site to its current literal (ruling R2). Two adjacent defects are fixed while the files are open: the duplicated `tasks.css` import, and the two unguarded `localStorage` reads at module scope in `main.ts`.

Design source: `map-design.md` sections 1.1 to 1.10 and section 4, plus `docs/design/idlescape-shell-v2/tokens/*.css` read directly.

**Files:**
- Modify: `web/src/styles/tokens.css` (85 lines, whole file), `web/src/styles/base.css` (43 lines), `web/src/styles/index.css` (14 lines), `web/src/main.ts:50-51`, `web/src/frame/stage.ts:241-247`
- Modify (mechanical freeze, R2): `web/src/styles/layout.css`, `button.css`, `forms.css`, `data.css`, `tasks.css` (**174 lines at HEAD, not 108: SP4b Task 11's `.report*` block and Task 10's banner detail rules are new, and both carry `var(--sp-*)` and `var(--fs-*)` call sites the freeze has to reach**), `frame.css`, `tabs.css`, `overlays.css`, `dialog.css`, `bank.css`
- Create: `web/src/styles/motion.css`, `web/src/styles/tokens.test.ts`
- Test: `web/src/styles/tokens.test.ts` (create), `web/src/frame/stage.test.ts` (modify)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing from earlier tasks.
- Produces, and every later task depends on these names:
  - The token set of `map-design.md` sections 1.1 to 1.9, verbatim from `docs/design/idlescape-shell-v2/tokens/{colors,typography,spacing,motion}.css`, plus the R18 and geometry tokens listed in Step 3.
  - `web/src/styles/motion.css` defining `pulse`, `shimmer`, `barflow`, `floatUp`, `fadeUp`, `popIn`, `bob`, `stepPulse`.
  - `web/src/frame/stage.ts` exports `export const PANEL_W = 280;` and `export const STRIP_W = 40;`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing token test**

Create `web/src/styles/tokens.test.ts`. It reads stylesheet text rather than a rendered DOM, because jsdom does not resolve custom properties across an `@import`.

```ts
// web/src/styles/tokens.test.ts -- pins the v2 token layer to the vendored bundle. Every value
// here was copied from docs/design/idlescape-shell-v2/tokens/*.css; when the two disagree the
// bundle wins and this file is what says so out loud.
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const read = (name: string): string => readFileSync(join(here, name), 'utf8');
const bundle = (name: string): string =>
  readFileSync(join(here, '../../../docs/design/idlescape-shell-v2/tokens', name), 'utf8');

/** `--x: value;` out of a stylesheet, first declaration wins. */
function tokenValue(css: string, name: string): string | null {
  const m = new RegExp(`--${name}\\s*:\\s*([^;]+);`).exec(css);
  return m ? m[1].trim() : null;
}

describe('tokens.css', () => {
  const css = read('tokens.css');

  it('carries every colour token the bundle declares, at the bundle value', () => {
    const colors = bundle('colors.css');
    for (const name of ['ground', 'frame', 'sunken', 'bar', 'panel', 'card', 'control', 'control-hover',
      'hairline', 'hairline-soft', 'hairline-strong', 'edge',
      'text-strong', 'text', 'text-muted', 'text-faint', 'text-disabled',
      'accent', 'accent-bright', 'accent-deep', 'on-accent', 'accent-tint', 'accent-glow',
      'ok', 'ok-bright', 'ok-pop', 'warn', 'error', 'danger', 'danger-deep', 'danger-hover',
      'info', 'info-bright', 'hp', 'hp-bright', 'prayer', 'prayer-bright', 'run', 'run-bright',
      'focus-ring', 'overlay-scrim']) {
      expect(tokenValue(css, name), name).toBe(tokenValue(colors, name));
    }
  });

  it('carries the spacing scale at the bundle values, not the old 4px rhythm', () => {
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'sp-1')).toBe('2px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'sp-2')).toBe('3px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'sp-3')).toBe('4px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'sp-4')).toBe('6px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'sp-5')).toBe('8px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'sp-6')).toBe('10px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'sp-7')).toBe('12px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'sp-8')).toBe('14px');
  });

  it('carries the type scale at the bundle values', () => {
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'fs-label')).toBe('9.5px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'fs-micro')).toBe('10px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'fs-tiny')).toBe('10.5px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'fs-small')).toBe('11px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'fs-base')).toBe('12px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'fs-md')).toBe('12.5px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'fs-emph')).toBe('13.5px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'fs-title')).toBe('14.5px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'fs-brand')).toBe('16px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'lh')).toBe('1.45');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'font-num')).toBe('ui-monospace, Menlo, Consolas, monospace');
    // The bundle drops "Press Start 2P" from the pixel stack.
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'font-pixel')).toBe('"Pixelify Sans", ui-monospace, monospace');
  });

  it('carries the four control heights and the frame geometry', () => {
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'ctl-h-xs')).toBe('22px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'ctl-h-sm')).toBe('25px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'ctl-h')).toBe('27px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'ctl-h-lg')).toBe('29px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'panel-w')).toBe('280px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'strip-w')).toBe('40px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'title-h')).toBe('28px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'foot-h')).toBe('21px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'tabs-h')).toBe('32px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'bar-h')).toBe('42px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'panel-head-h')).toBe('34px');
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'strip-btn-h')).toBe('33px');
  });

  it('keeps the layer tokens and the dark colour scheme the bundle drops', () => {
    // The bundle hard-codes z-index:25 on the bank window and 30 on the trace window. The shell
    // has had a layer scale since SP1 and the two windows are the reason it exists.
    for (const name of ['z-overlay', 'z-panel', 'z-toast', 'z-dialog']) expect(tokenValue(css, name)).not.toBeNull();
    expect(css).toContain('color-scheme: dark');
  });

  it('replaces the two-ring focus halo with the single orange wash', () => {
    expect(tokenValue(css, 'focus-ring')).toBe('0 0 0 3px rgba(255,152,31,.18)');
  });

  it('tokenises the colours the mock hard-codes (ruling R18)', () => {
    const pairs: [string, string][] = [
      ['chrome-bar', '#1b1b1d'], ['chrome-tab-active', '#28282a'], ['chrome-hover', '#2a2a2c'],
      ['track', '#2c2c2e'], ['kbd-border', '#3e3e3c'], ['dot-idle', '#666666'],
      ['glyph-rest', '#9a9a98'], ['text-quiet', '#a0a09e'], ['error-text', '#f07370'],
      ['xp-chip', '#2e7d32'], ['count-stack', '#ffff00'], ['count-large', '#00ff80'],
      ['bank-ground', '#252220'], ['bank-head', '#2c2925'], ['bank-tab-active', '#33302c'],
      ['bank-well', '#171513'], ['bank-dash', '#4e4a44'],
      ['shot-a', '#141414'], ['shot-b', '#191919'], ['canvas-a', '#0a0a0a'], ['canvas-b', '#0d0d0d']
    ];
    for (const [name, value] of pairs) expect(tokenValue(css, name), name).toBe(value);
  });
});

describe('the legacy stylesheets are frozen while the tokens change under them (ruling R2)', () => {
  const LEGACY = ['layout.css', 'button.css', 'forms.css', 'data.css', 'tasks.css', 'frame.css',
    'tabs.css', 'overlays.css', 'dialog.css', 'bank.css'];
  // --title-h and --foot-h already exist at 22px/18px and frame.css already reads them, so they
  // belong here as much as --sp-N does. --panel-w and --strip-w are deliberately ABSENT: ruling
  // R3 makes frame.css's two width declarations the named exceptions to the freeze.
  const CHANGED = /var\(--(sp-[1-6]|fs-base|fs-md|fs-lg|fs-xl|fs-2xl|lh|lh-tight|ctl-h|ctl-h-sm|ctl-h-lg|title-h|foot-h|ease|dur-fast|dur-base|dur-slow|radius|radius-lg)\)/;

  it.each(LEGACY)('%s uses no token whose value changed', name => {
    const found = CHANGED.exec(read(name));
    expect(found?.[0] ?? null).toBeNull();
  });

  it('keeps the two frame widths on their tokens, because the canvas reserves them (ruling R3)', () => {
    const frame = read('frame.css');
    expect(frame).toContain('width: var(--panel-w)');
    expect(frame).toContain('width: var(--strip-w)');
  });

  // Decisions D26 and D48: the merged z-scale is six named lanes and nothing else. A later entry
  // that wants a new floating surface takes a lane; a ladder of per-window numbers is the thing
  // this case exists to stop. Mutation target: adding `--z-studio: 35` fails the second
  // assertion, and reordering two lanes fails the first.
  it('declares the six stacking lanes in ascending order and no others', () => {
    const lanes = ['z-overlay', 'z-panel', 'z-window', 'z-stage-window', 'z-toast', 'z-dialog'];
    const values = lanes.map(n => Number(tokenValue(css, n)));
    expect(values).toEqual([5, 10, 20, 30, 40, 50]);
    expect([...css.matchAll(/--z-[a-z-]+\s*:/g)].map(m => m[0])).toHaveLength(lanes.length);
  });
});

describe('motion.css', () => {
  const css = read('motion.css');
  it('defines all eight keyframes', () => {
    for (const k of ['pulse', 'shimmer', 'barflow', 'floatUp', 'fadeUp', 'popIn', 'bob', 'stepPulse']) {
      expect(css).toContain(`@keyframes ${k}`);
    }
  });
  it('disables animation under reduced motion, in exactly one place', () => {
    expect(css).toContain('prefers-reduced-motion: reduce');
    expect(css).toContain('animation-iteration-count: 1 !important');
    expect(read('base.css')).not.toContain('prefers-reduced-motion');
  });
});

describe('main.ts', () => {
  it('never reads localStorage unguarded at module scope', () => {
    const main = readFileSync(join(here, '../main.ts'), 'utf8');
    // Mutation target: restoring `const sizeMode = localStorage.getItem('cs.size')` fails this.
    expect(main).not.toMatch(/^(const|let)\s+\w+[^\n]*localStorage\.getItem/m);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the v2 `tokens.css`**

Replace `web/src/styles/tokens.css` entirely. Copy the four bundle token files verbatim into one `:root`, in the bundle's own order (colors, typography, spacing, motion), then add the three groups the bundle does not carry. Do **not** copy typography.css's `@import url(https://fonts.googleapis.com/...)`: the font is already linked from `web/index.html:11` and `web/styleguide.html:10`, the two pages that render the wordmark, and a CSS-level `@import` inside a bundled stylesheet is a second, render-blocking request for the same file. `web/play.html` carries neither the preconnect nor the stylesheet and **needs no link**: it is the untouched client frame and the canvas is never restyled. Do not add one there.

Keep `color-scheme: dark`. **The four `--z-*` tokens are kept but no longer sufficient, and this step is where the merged z-scale gets an owner (decisions D26 and D48).** Today they are `--z-overlay: 5`, `--z-panel: 10`, `--z-toast: 40`, `--z-dialog: 50` (`tokens.css:75-78`), and the two floating surfaces that exist do not sit on that scale together: `tasks.css:111` puts the run banner at `calc(var(--z-overlay) + 1)` while `bank.css:16` puts `.bank-host` at `calc(var(--z-dialog) - 1)`, which is 49. A trace window written at `--z-overlay + 2`, which is what Task 16 said before this pass, would therefore render **under** the bank window, which is the opposite of what the mock draws and of what Task 7's own note claimed ("the relative order, trace above bank, is what the mock is expressing"). Two named lanes settle it, and every later entry reads them rather than inventing a number:

```css
  /* The stacking scale, as five named lanes. Nothing in the shell writes a bare z-index; a new
     floating surface takes one of these or it is a design question, not a CSS one.
     --z-window is the lane for a window docked over the canvas column; --z-stage-window is the
     lane for a window over the whole stage. There is deliberately no third window lane: two
     stage windows are ordered by DOM order, with the most recently opened appended last, which
     is how the stage already orders siblings (decision D48). */
  --z-overlay: 5;          /* canvas pills, the HUD stack, the xp drop */
  --z-panel: 10;           /* side panel chrome and strip tooltips */
  --z-window: 20;          /* the co-pilot bar and anything docked in the canvas column */
  --z-stage-window: 30;    /* .window: the bank window, the trace pop-out, the Script Studio */
  --z-toast: 40;
  --z-dialog: 50;          /* <dialog>, which is the only thing above a stage window */
```

`bank.css:16`'s `calc(var(--z-dialog) - 1)` becomes `var(--z-stage-window)` in Task 17, and `tasks.css:111`'s `calc(var(--z-overlay) + 1)` becomes `var(--z-window)` when Task 14 replaces the banner with the bar. Until then both are frozen at their current literals by Step 6 like every other legacy declaration, so this step adds the names and changes nothing on screen.

- [ ] **Step 4: Create `motion.css`**

`web/src/styles/motion.css`, copied verbatim from `docs/design/idlescape-shell-v2/tokens/motion.css` minus its `:root` line (the durations live in `tokens.css`):

```css
/* idlescape motion. Answers an action; off under reduced motion. Copied from the vendored
   bundle at docs/design/idlescape-shell-v2/tokens/motion.css; do not retune by hand.
   shimmer and barflow have identical bodies on purpose: both consumers set
   background-size:200% 100% on the element, and the two names differ only in intent. */
@keyframes pulse { 50% { box-shadow: 0 0 0 6px rgba(255,152,31,.10); } }
@keyframes shimmer { to { background-position: -200% 0; } }
@keyframes barflow { to { background-position: -200% 0; } }
@keyframes floatUp { 0% { opacity:0; transform:translateY(8px) scale(.9);} 15% {opacity:1; transform:translateY(0) scale(1);} 75% {opacity:1;} 100% {opacity:0; transform:translateY(-30px);} }
@keyframes fadeUp { from { opacity:0; transform:translateY(6px); } }
@keyframes popIn { from { opacity:0; transform:scale(.94); } }
@keyframes bob { 50% { transform:translateY(-5px); } }
@keyframes stepPulse { 50% { box-shadow: 0 0 0 4px rgba(255,152,31,.15); } }
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  *, *::before, *::after { animation-duration: .01ms !important; animation-iteration-count: 1 !important; transition-duration: .01ms !important; }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Fold the bundle's base layer into `base.css`**

Keep everything `base.css` has that the bundle does not contradict: the `box-sizing` reset, `.hidden`, `.sr-only`, `:focus-visible`, the `::-webkit-scrollbar` rules, `.muted`, `.pixel`, `.num` and `kbd`.

One line inside the kept block has to change with the tokens: `base.css:26` is `:focus-visible { box-shadow: var(--focus-ring); border-radius: var(--radius); }` and Step 3 deletes `--radius`. Restate it as the bundle's control radius, so the ring keeps its corners:

```css
:focus-visible { box-shadow: var(--focus-ring); border-radius: var(--r-ctl); }
```

`--r-ctl` is one of the four names the fold keeps reaching for out of `tokens.css` (`--r-ctl`, `--focus-ring`, `--kbd-border`, `--font-num`); none of them is legacy and none is frozen. Replace the body font and colour, the `a`, `b`, `button`, `input` and `::selection` rules with the bundle's, and delete the reduced-motion block (it moved to `motion.css`).

```css
html, body {
  margin: 0;
  background: var(--frame);
  color: var(--text);
  font: var(--fs-base)/var(--lh) var(--font-ui);
  -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
  text-rendering: optimizeLegibility;
}
a { color: var(--accent); text-decoration: none; }
a:hover { text-decoration: underline; }
b, strong { color: var(--text-strong); font-weight: 600; }
button { cursor: pointer; font: inherit; transition: background var(--dur) var(--ease), border-color var(--dur) var(--ease), color var(--dur) var(--ease), box-shadow var(--dur) var(--ease), transform var(--dur) var(--ease), opacity var(--dur) var(--ease); }
input, textarea, select { font: inherit; color: inherit; transition: border-color var(--dur) var(--ease), box-shadow var(--dur) var(--ease); }
input:focus, textarea:focus, select:focus { outline: none; border-color: var(--accent) !important; box-shadow: var(--focus-ring); }
::selection { background: rgba(255,152,31,.35); color: var(--text-strong); }
* { scrollbar-width: thin; scrollbar-color: #3c3c3c var(--bar); }
kbd { display: inline-block; padding: 1px 6px; border: 1px solid var(--kbd-border); border-bottom-width: 2px; border-radius: 4px; background: var(--card); color: var(--text-strong); font: var(--fs-micro)/1.6 var(--font-ui); }
```

The `kbd` rule takes the mock's co-pilot bar values (`padding:1px 6px`, `border-radius:4px`, `background:#232325`, `font-size:10px`), because that is the only `kbd` the shell renders and the mock styles it inline. The bundle's `scrollbar-color` is `#3c3c3c var(--bar)` and the mock's inline copy drifts to `#1a1a1a`; the bundle wins, being the token layer. The mock's `::selection` colour drifts to `#e6e6e6`, the old strong text; take `--text-strong`.

- [ ] **Step 9: Read the geometry from the tokens**

`web/src/frame/stage.ts`, above `createStage`:

```ts
/**
 * Frame geometry, in one place. The tokens in styles/tokens.css are the source of truth; these
 * constants are the fallback for an environment that cannot compute them (jsdom returns the empty
 * string for a custom property read off documentElement), and stage.test.ts pins them to the
 * token literals so the two can never drift apart silently.
 */
export const PANEL_W = 280;
export const STRIP_W = 40;

function pxToken(name: string, fallback: number): number {
  const raw = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue(name).trim();
  const n = Number.parseFloat(raw);
  return Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0 ? n : fallback;
}
```

and in `layout()` replace the hardcoded arithmetic:

```ts
    const reserved = pxToken('--strip-w', STRIP_W)
      + (panelOpen && stage.clientWidth > 1100 ? pxToken('--panel-w', PANEL_W) : 0)
      + 16;
```

Run: `cd web && npx vitest run src/frame/stage.test.ts` - expect PASS.

- [ ] **Step 10: Guard the two module-scope `localStorage` reads**

`web/src/main.ts:50-51` reads `cs.size` and `cs.filter` unguarded, which throws outright in a private window with site data blocked and takes the whole shell down before anything renders.

```ts
function readLocal(key: string): string | null {
  try { return localStorage.getItem(key); } catch { return null; }
}
let sizeMode = (readLocal('cs.size') as SizeMode | null) ?? 'auto';
let filter = (readLocal('cs.filter') as 'auto' | 'pixelated' | null) ?? 'auto';
```

**Keep the names.** `main.ts:51` declares `let filter`, read at `:116` (`getFilter: () => filter`) and written at `:145-146` (`setFilter`); renaming it to `filterMode` breaks three call sites this step does not list. `sizeMode` is already its own name.

Run: `cd web && npx vitest run src/styles/tokens.test.ts` - the `main.ts` case goes green.

Everything else renders exactly as it does today. If `partials.test.ts` fails on a `.title-link` regex, the substitution changed a rule it matches by regex - fix the regex, not the CSS.

### Task 2: The CSS split, and the styles rule with teeth

G9's split, and library part 4 in the same task because `map-tests.md` proves the rule is a hard dependency of the split rather than a finisher: thirty `.p-*` classes have roughly 180 emission sites and exactly five test assertions, so a half-finished retirement passes the whole gate. The rule is what makes the retirement checkable.

Component families live at `web/src/styles/<family>.css` and own one class prefix set each. Per-surface geometry lives at `web/src/styles/layout/<surface>.css` and may not open a selector with a family class. `.p-*` survives this task, moved intact into `web/src/styles/legacy.css`, which the rule marks as a shrinking allowlist; Task 3 deletes it.

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/styles/badge.css`, `card.css`, `meter.css`, `alert.css`, `overlay.css`, `strip.css`, `tab.css`, `icon.css`, `legacy.css`, `families.ts`, `styles.test.ts`
- Create: `web/src/styles/layout/frame.css`, `layout/panels.css`, `layout/automation.css`, `layout/trace.css`, `layout/bank.css`, `layout/dialog.css`
- Rename: `web/src/styles/forms.css` to `form.css`
- Modify: `web/src/styles/index.css`, `button.css`

- [ ] **Step 0: Free the name `.card`, which the gate screen already owns**

`web/src/styles/layout.css:15` defines `.card` as the full-screen centred card of the gate, home and character-creation screens (`max-width:360px`, `--radius-lg`, `--sp-5 --sp-4 --sp-4` padding), with `.card-screen`, `.card-wide`, `.card-title`, `.card-heading`, `.card-sub` and `.card-foot` around it. Task 6 then writes the v2 panel card, `padding:11px; background: var(--card); border-radius: var(--r-card)`, under the same name. The later rule wins on padding, background, border and radius, and the gate, home and character-creation screens silently take panel-card chrome. No test covers those three screens' card, so nothing would catch it.

Rename the screen family off the `card` prefix entirely, before anything else in this task, so `card.css` can be the component and the screen chrome can stay per-surface geometry where it belongs:

```
.card-screen  -> .screen          .card-title   -> .screen-title
.card         -> .screen-card     .card-heading -> .screen-heading
.card-wide    -> .screen-card-wide  .card-sub   -> .screen-sub
.card-foot    -> .screen-foot
```

Call sites, all of them, re-anchored at HEAD: `web/src/partials/gate.html:1,2,3,4`, `home.html:1,2,3,4,51,52,57,58`, `characters.html:1,2,3,4,5,14`, `web/styleguide.html:120,312,450,451,452,458,459`, and the one `.card-foot` in `web/src/plugins/builtin/tasks.ts:310` (the marketplace footer, which Task 10 rewrites into a tab anyway). `.card-wide` has no call site at all outside `layout.css:23`; rename the rule and expect no consumer. Grep `card-screen|card-title|card-heading|card-sub|card-wide|card-foot|class="card"` across `web/src` and `web/styleguide.html` and confirm the only survivors are `marketplace.ts:52`'s `class: 'card market-card'` and `bank.css`'s own `.card` consumers, which are the component and not the screen.

The renamed rules go to `layout/panels.css` in Step 3, not to `card.css`: `screen` is not a family prefix, so the layout rule accepts them, and the screens are per-surface geometry by every definition this task uses. Commit this rename on its own, before the split, so a reviewer sees a pure rename:

```bash
git add web/src/styles/layout.css web/src/partials web/styleguide.html web/src/plugins/builtin/tasks.ts
git -c core.safecrlf=false commit -m "refactor(styles): rename the gate screen card off the .card prefix

Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5.1 <noreply@anthropic.com>
Claude-Session: 6cfc8eb7-fa0f-4398-8701-64d1005b1577"
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the family table**

`web/src/styles/families.ts`:

```ts
// web/src/styles/families.ts -- the component library's shape, as data.
//
// One file per component family, and each file owns exactly the class prefixes listed here.
// styles.test.ts enforces it, which is the mechanical half of "every new panel composes these
// classes; no per-panel one-off styles": there is nowhere else to put one.
export const FAMILIES: Record<string, readonly string[]> = {
  'button.css': ['btn', 'link'],
  'badge.css': ['badge', 'tag', 'dot'],
  // `section` and not just `section-label`: layout.css's collapsible `.section` / `.section-body`
  // move here in Step 3, and `owns()` matches only a prefix or `prefix-`, so `section-label`
  // alone would reject `.section`. Choosing this rather than routing `.section*` to a layout
  // file, because a collapsible section is a data-family component, not a per-surface shape.
  'card.css': ['card', 'kv', 'section', 'table'],
  'meter.css': ['meter', 'shimmer'],
  'form.css': ['input', 'select', 'textarea', 'field', 'switch', 'code'],
  'alert.css': ['alert', 'empty', 'toast'],
  'overlay.css': ['ov', 'hud', 'xp-drop', 'window'],
  'strip.css': ['icon-strip', 'strip'],
  'tab.css': ['char-tab', 'char-tabs', 'seg', 'chip'],
  'icon.css': ['icon']
};

/** Per-surface geometry only. A layout file may position family classes but may not restyle one. */
export const LAYOUT_DIR = 'layout';

/** Shrinking allowlist. Task 3 deletes this file and this entry with it. */
export const LEGACY_FILE = 'legacy.css';
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing styles rule**

`web/src/styles/styles.test.ts`. Five rules, each with a named mutation target.

```ts
// web/src/styles/styles.test.ts -- the rule with teeth (companion spec section 5, part 4).
import { readdirSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { FAMILIES, LAYOUT_DIR, LEGACY_FILE } from './families';

const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const read = (rel: string): string => readFileSync(join(here, rel), 'utf8');
const cssFiles = (dir: string): string[] =>
  readdirSync(join(here, dir)).filter(f => f.endsWith('.css')).map(f => (dir === '.' ? f : `${dir}/${f}`));

/** Strip comments, then take every `.class` token out of every selector, in order. */
function rulesOf(css: string): { selector: string; classes: string[] }[] {
  const clean = css.replace(/\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\//g, '');
  const out: { selector: string; classes: string[] }[] = [];
  for (const m of clean.matchAll(/([^{}]+)\{[^{}]*\}/g)) {
    const selector = m[1].trim();
    if (!selector || selector.startsWith('@') || selector.startsWith(':root') || selector.startsWith('%')) continue;
    out.push({ selector, classes: [...selector.matchAll(/\.([a-zA-Z][\w-]*)/g)].map(c => c[1]) });
  }
  return out;
}
const owns = (prefixes: readonly string[], cls: string): boolean =>
  prefixes.some(p => cls === p || cls.startsWith(`${p}-`));

describe('the component library owns its own classes', () => {
  it.each(Object.keys(FAMILIES))('%s defines only its own family', name => {
    const prefixes = FAMILIES[name];
    for (const { selector, classes } of rulesOf(read(name))) {
      // The FIRST class in a selector is the thing being styled; later ones are context.
      const subject = classes[0];
      if (subject === undefined) continue;
      expect(owns(prefixes, subject), `${name}: "${selector}" styles .${subject}`).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it('no layout file opens a selector with a library class', () => {
    const owned = Object.values(FAMILIES).flat();
    for (const file of cssFiles(LAYOUT_DIR)) {
      for (const { selector, classes } of rulesOf(read(file))) {
        const subject = classes[0];
        if (subject === undefined) continue;
        // A layout file may target a family class as a DESCENDANT (".events-feed .card"), which
        // puts a layout class first. It may not open a selector with one.
        expect(owns(owned, subject), `${file}: "${selector}" restyles the library class .${subject}`).toBe(false);
      }
    }
  });

  it('every stylesheet on disk is imported exactly once', () => {
    const imported = [...read('index.css').matchAll(/@import '\.\/([^']+)';/g)].map(m => m[1]);
    expect(new Set(imported).size, 'a stylesheet is imported twice').toBe(imported.length);
    const onDisk = [...cssFiles('.'), ...cssFiles(LAYOUT_DIR)].filter(f => f !== 'index.css');
    expect([...imported].sort()).toEqual([...onDisk].sort());
  });

  it('every stylesheet is under the 400 line ceiling', () => {
    for (const file of [...cssFiles('.'), ...cssFiles(LAYOUT_DIR)]) {
      expect(read(file).split('\n').length, file).toBeLessThan(400);
    }
  });

  it('the .p- dialect exists only in the shrinking legacy file', () => {
    for (const file of [...cssFiles('.'), ...cssFiles(LAYOUT_DIR)]) {
      if (file === LEGACY_FILE) continue;
      expect(read(file), file).not.toMatch(/\.p-[a-z]/);
    }
  });
});
```

Run: `cd web && npx vitest run src/styles/styles.test.ts`
Expected: FAIL on the import case first (`layout/` does not exist), then on the family cases.

`.session-row` (`data.css`) and the `.wiki-link` rule move to `layout/panels.css`: both are per-surface, and the styles rule will say so if you put them anywhere else.

Three rows above are the ones an implementer gets wrong by following the old table, and each one fails a rule this task writes:

- **`.toast*` lives in `overlays.css`, not `data.css`**, and `@keyframes toast-in` lives there with it. It is a ninth keyframe, and Task 1's `motion.css` test asserts the bundle's eight by name, so append `toast-in` to `motion.css` and add `'toast-in'` to that test's list in Step 4. Deleting `overlays.css` without moving the keyframe silently kills the toast entry animation, which `createToastHost` uses on every notify and `plugins.pw` exercises.
- **`.overlays` goes to `layout/frame.css`, not `overlay.css`.** `overlay.css`'s prefixes are `ov`, `hud`, `xp-drop` and `window`; `overlays` is neither `ov` nor `ov-*`, so `overlay.css defines only its own family` fails on it. It is a frame-region container, which is what `layout/frame.css` is for, and it is not an owned class so the layout rule accepts it.
- **`.dot-accent` is in `tasks.css:167`, not `data.css`.** It matches none of the `tasks.css` rows, so following the table without this line drops it into `layout/automation.css`, where `no layout file opens a selector with a library class` fails on it: `dot-accent` is owned by `badge.css`. `.strip-btn { position: relative }` at `:168` goes to `strip.css` with `.strip-dot` at `:169` and the reduced-motion block at `:171-173`.
- **`.trace-head .p-input` (`tasks.css:68`) is the one `.p-` rule outside the three alias files**, added by SP4b Task 11 to make the trace's kind select fill its row. `layout/trace.css` is neither the legacy file nor exempt, so leaving it there fails `the .p- dialect exists only in the shrinking legacy file` on a file this task creates. Move that one declaration block into `legacy.css` beside the aliases; Task 3 Step 2 renames it to `.trace-head .input` and returns it to `layout/trace.css` in the same pass that renames the emitter at `traceView.ts:135`.

Also move the `.p-muted` alias out of `base.css:36` and leave `.muted` behind. `base.css` is neither in the move table nor the legacy file, so the `.p-` rule Step 2 writes fails on it otherwise, and the failure names a file this task claims not to touch.

/* idlescape web shell stylesheet. Order matters: tokens, base, motion, then the component
